--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { runBatch, type BatchUnit } from "@/lib/batch/batch-coordinator";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

interface BatchRequest {
  units: { id: string; group: string; safeForParallel: boolean; input: Record<string, unknown> }[];
  maxConcurrent?: number;
}

/**
 * POST /api/batch — runs a synthetic batch of inert "echo" units.
 * Real callers supply unit definitions through internal modules; this HTTP
 * surface is for smoke/contract testing only.
 */
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const body = (await req.json()) as BatchRequest;
  if (!body?.units?.length) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "units required" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const units: BatchUnit<Record<string, unknown>>[] = body.units.map((u) => ({
    id: u.id,
    group: u.group,
    safeForParallel: u.safeForParallel,
    input: u.input,
    run: async (input) => ({ echo: input, ranAt: new Date().toISOString() }),
  }));
  const report = await runBatch(units, { maxConcurrent: body.maxConcurrent ?? 4 });
  return NextResponse.json(report);
}

export interface BatchUnit<T = unknown> {
  id: string;

  dependsOn?: string[];
}

```
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  runBatch,
  type BatchUnit,
  type BatchGroup,
} from "@/lib/batch/batch-coordinator";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

interface BatchRequest {
  units: { id: string; groupId: string; safeForParallel: boolean; payload: Record<string, unknown> }[];
  groups?: { id: string; units: string[]; dependsOn?: string[] }[];
  concurrencyLimit?: number;
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const body = (await req.json()) as BatchRequest;
  if (!body?.units?.length) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "units required" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const units: BatchUnit<Record<string, unknown>>[] = body.units.map((u) => ({
    id: u.id,
    groupId: u.groupId,
    safeForParallel: u.safeForParallel,
    payload: u.payload,
  }));

  const groups: BatchGroup[] = body.groups ?? [
    { id: "default", units: units.map((u) => u.id) },
  ];

  const report = await runBatch(
    units,
    groups,
    async (unit) => ({ echo: unit.payload, ranAt: new Date().toISOString() }),
    { concurrencyLimit: body.concurrencyLimit ?? 4 }
  );

  return NextResponse.json(report);
}
```

export function startCompilerJob(
  projectId: string,
  opts: { targetId?: string; idempotencyKey?: string; retryPolicy?: RetryPolicy } = {}
): CompilerJob {
  const job = durableCreateJob(projectId, {
    targetId: opts.targetId,
    idempotencyKey: opts.idempotencyKey ?? uuidv4(),
    retryPolicy: opts.retryPolicy ?? DEFAULT_RETRY_POLICY,
  });
  defaultPool.acquire(() => runJob(job)).catch(() => {});
  return job;
}

export function getCompilerJob(jobId: string, projectId: string): CompilerJob | undefined {
  return durableGetJob(jobId, projectId);

/**
 * TFA Evolution Studio v28 — Event Store
 *
 * Append-only event log for platform observability.
 * Supports project-scoped and global event streams.
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid";
import { getTracesDir, ensureDir } from "@/lib/paths";
import type { PlatformEvent } from "@/lib/types";

function getEventsFile(projectId?: string): string {
  if (projectId) {
    return path.join(getTracesDir(), `${projectId}.ndjson`);
  }
  return path.join(getTracesDir(), "global.ndjson");
}

function loadEvents(projectId?: string): PlatformEvent[] {
  const filePath = getEventsFile(projectId);
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return [];
  const lines = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf-8").split("\n").filter(Boolean);
  return lines.map((line) => JSON.parse(line) as PlatformEvent);
}

function appendEvent(event: PlatformEvent, projectId?: string): void {
  const filePath = getEventsFile(projectId);
  ensureDir(path.dirname(filePath));

<action>
**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-cl2omik5tds2/src/lib/event-store/event-store.ts]
Thought: Update `getProjectEvents` stub to accept an options object, and rebuild:
Command: str_replace
Old String: ```
export function getProjectEvents(projectId: string, limit = 500) {
  return getEvents(projectId, limit);
}
```
New String: ```
export function getProjectEvents(
  projectId: string,
  opts?: { limit?: number; after?: string; kinds?: string[] }
) {
  let events = getEvents(projectId);
  if (opts?.kinds?.length) events = events.filter((e) => opts.kinds!.includes((e as { type?: string }).type ?? ""));
  if (opts?.after) events = events.filter((e) => e.timestamp > opts.after!);
  if (opts?.limit) events = events.slice(-opts.limit);
  return events;
}
```

export function recordEvent(
  type: string,
  payload: unknown,
  projectId?: string,
  runId?: string
): PlatformEvent {
  const event: PlatformEvent = {
    id: uuidv4(),
    projectId,
    runId,
    type,
    payload,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  appendEvent(event, projectId);
  return event;
}

```
export function emitEvent(
  opts: { projectId?: string; kind?: string; severity?: string; payload?: unknown } | string,
  kind?: string,
  payload?: unknown
): void {
  if (typeof opts === "string") {
    recordEvent({ projectId: opts, kind: kind as Parameters<typeof recordEvent>[0]["kind"], payload });

```
New String: ```
export function emitEvent(
  opts: Record<string, unknown> | string,
  kind?: string,
  payload?: unknown
): PlatformEvent {
  if (typeof opts === "string") {
    return recordEvent(kind ?? "unknown", payload, opts);
  }
  return recordEvent(
    (opts.kind ?? opts.type ?? "unknown") as string,
    opts.payload,
    opts.projectId as string | undefined,
    opts.runId as string | undefined
  );
}
```

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { runEvolutionCycle } from "@/lib/evolution-engine";

export async function POST() {
  try {
    const run = await runEvolutionCycle();
    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      runId: run.id,
      status: run.status,
      targetVersionId: run.targetVersionId,
      progress: run.progress,
      logs: run.logs,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Evolution cycle failed";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// Thin coordinator — delegates to phases, does not contain logic itself.

export function runEvolutionCycle(projectId: string, prompt?: string): CycleEvidenceChain {
  // Phase 1: Prepare
  const { runId, chain, workspaceDir } = prepareEvolutionCycle(projectId);

  // Phase 2: Validate
  const validation = validateEvolutionInputs(projectId, prompt, chain);
  if (!validation.valid) {
    chain.passRate = 0;
    chain.complete = true;
    chain.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return chain;
  }

  // Phase 3: Load
  const load = loadProjectContext(projectId, workspaceDir, chain);

  // Phase 4: Analyze
  const analysis = analyzeProjectState(projectId, load.detectedAdapter, chain);

  // Phase 5: Plan
  const plan = planEvolutionSteps(projectId, prompt, analysis, chain);
[The command completed with exit code 0.]

```
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { runEvolutionCycle } from "@/lib/evolution-engine";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const projectId: string = (body as { projectId?: string }).projectId ?? "__platform__";
    const prompt: string | undefined = (body as { prompt?: string }).prompt;
    const chain = runEvolutionCycle(projectId, prompt);
    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      runId: chain.runId,
      projectId: chain.projectId,
      passRate: chain.passRate,
      complete: chain.complete,
      completedAt: chain.completedAt,
      nodeCount: chain.nodes.length,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Evolution cycle failed";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { runEvolutionCycle } from "@/lib/evolution-engine";

export const maxDuration = 300;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const projectId: string = (body as { projectId?: string }).projectId ?? "__platform__";
    const prompt: string | undefined = (body as { prompt?: string }).prompt;
    const chain = runEvolutionCycle(projectId, prompt);
    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      runId: chain.runId,
      projectId: chain.projectId,
      passRate: chain.passRate,
      complete: chain.complete,
      completedAt: chain.completedAt,
      nodeCount: chain.nodes.length,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Evolution cycle failed";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { getActivePipelineRun, subscribePipelineRun } from "@/lib/engine/pipeline-engine";
import { getActiveRun, subscribeRun } from "@/lib/orchestrator";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ runId: string }> }) {
  const { runId } = await ctx.params;

  // Support both v1 (orchestrator) and v2 (pipeline engine) runs
  const run = getActivePipelineRun(runId) ?? getActiveRun(runId);
  if (!run) return new Response("Run not found", { status: 404 });

  const subscribeFn = getActivePipelineRun(runId) ? subscribePipelineRun : subscribeRun;

  const stream = new ReadableStream({
    start(controller) {
      const enc = new TextEncoder();
      const send = (data: unknown) =>
        controller.enqueue(enc.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`));

      send({ type: "snapshot", run });

      const unsub = subscribeFn(runId, (entry) => {
        send({ type: "log", entry, run: { ...run, logs: undefined } });
        if (run.status === "complete" || run.status === "failed") {
          send({ type: "done", run });
          // Clear the polling interval immediately so it does not fire again
          // after the subscriber has already closed the stream.
          clearInterval(interval);
          unsub();
          try { controller.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        }
      });

      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        if (run.status === "complete" || run.status === "failed") {
          send({ type: "done", run });
          clearInterval(interval);
          unsub();
          try { controller.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        }
      }, 2000);

      req.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
        clearInterval(interval);
        unsub();
        try { controller.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
      });
    },
  });

  return new Response(stream, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
      "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
      Connection: "keep-alive",
    },
  });
}

import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { getActivePipelineRun, subscribePipelineRun } from "@/lib/engine/pipeline-engine";
import { getActiveRun, subscribeRun } from "@/lib/orchestrator";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

type AnyRun = { complete?: boolean; status?: string };

function isRunDone(run: AnyRun): boolean {
  if (run.complete === true) return true;
  if (run.status === "complete" || run.status === "completed" || run.status === "failed") return true;
  return false;
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ runId: string }> }) {
  const { runId } = await ctx.params;

  const run = (getActivePipelineRun(runId) ?? getActiveRun(runId)) as AnyRun | undefined;
  if (!run) return new Response("Run not found", { status: 404 });

  const subscribeFn = getActivePipelineRun(runId) ? subscribePipelineRun : subscribeRun;

  const stream = new ReadableStream({
    start(controller) {
      const enc = new TextEncoder();
      const send = (data: unknown) =>
        controller.enqueue(enc.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`));

      send({ type: "snapshot", run });

      const unsub = (subscribeFn as (id: string, cb: (entry: unknown) => void) => () => void)(runId, (entry) => {
        send({ type: "log", entry, run });
        if (isRunDone(run)) {
          send({ type: "done", run });
          clearInterval(interval);
          unsub();
          try { controller.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        }
      });

      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        if (isRunDone(run)) {
          send({ type: "done", run });
          clearInterval(interval);
          unsub();
          try { controller.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        }
      }, 2000);

      req.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
        clearInterval(interval);
        unsub();
        try { controller.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
      });
    },
  });

  return new Response(stream, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
      "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
      Connection: "keep-alive",
    },
  });
}
ROUTEEOF

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  getApprovals,
  approveAction,
  rejectAction,
  requestApproval,
} from "@/lib/governance/approval-gate";
import { getAuditTrail, getAuditSummary } from "@/lib/governance/audit-trail";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const view = searchParams.get("view") ?? "approvals";
  const projectId = searchParams.get("projectId") ?? undefined;

  try {
    if (view === "audit") {
      const trail = getAuditTrail({ projectId, limit: 200 });
      const summary = getAuditSummary();
      return NextResponse.json({ trail, summary });
    }

    const status = searchParams.get("status") as "pending" | null;
    const approvals = getApprovals({ status: status ?? undefined, projectId, limit: 100 });
    return NextResponse.json({ approvals });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({})) as {
    action?: string;
    approvalId?: string;
    rejectedBy?: string;
    reason?: string;
    approvedBy?: string;
    // For creating new approval requests
    requestAction?: string;
    description?: string;
    requestedBy?: string;
    projectId?: string;
  };

  try {
    if (body.action === "approve" && body.approvalId) {
      const result = approveAction(body.approvalId, body.approvedBy ?? "admin");
      return NextResponse.json({ approval: result });
    }
    if (body.action === "reject" && body.approvalId) {
      const result = rejectAction(body.approvalId, body.rejectedBy ?? "admin", body.reason ?? "");
      return NextResponse.json({ approval: result });
    }
    if (body.action === "request" && body.requestAction) {
      const result = requestApproval({
        action: body.requestAction,
        description: body.description ?? "",
        requestedBy: body.requestedBy ?? "user",
        projectId: body.projectId ?? "__global__",
      });
      return NextResponse.json({ approval: result });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "action required: approve | reject | request" }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export function maskSensitiveConfig(config: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
  const sensitiveKeys = ["token", "secret", "password", "apiKey", "api_key", "key"];
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(config).map(([k, v]) =>
      sensitiveKeys.some((s) => k.toLowerCase().includes(s)) ? [k, "***"] : [k, v]
    )
  );

export function markJobRunning(jobId: string): CompilerJob | undefined {
  return updateJobStatus(jobId, "running", { startedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
}

export function updateJobProgress(jobId: string, progress: number): CompilerJob | undefined {
  return updateJobStatus(jobId, "running", { progress } as Partial<CompilerJob>);
}

export function workerHeartbeat(jobId: string): CompilerJob | undefined {
  return updateJobStatus(jobId, "running", { lastHeartbeatAt: new Date().toISOString() } as Partial<CompilerJob>);
}

export function completeJob(jobId: string, outputPath?: string): CompilerJob | undefined {
  return updateJobStatus(jobId, "done", { outputPath, completedAt: new Date().toISOString() } as Partial<CompilerJob>);

// GET    /api/jobs/[jobId]         — get single job
// DELETE /api/jobs/[jobId]         — request cancellation
// PATCH  /api/jobs/[jobId]         — update progress
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  getJob,
  requestJobCancellation,
  updateJobProgress,
  workerHeartbeat,
  markJobRunning,
  completeJob,
  failJob,
  JobProgress,
} from "@/lib/job-engine/job-engine";

export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ jobId: string }> }) {
  const { jobId } = await params;
  const job = getJob(jobId);
  if (!job) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Job not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: job });
}

export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ jobId: string }> }) {
  const { jobId } = await params;
  const cancelled = requestJobCancellation(jobId);
  if (!cancelled) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Job not found or already terminal" }, { status: 404 });
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Cancellation requested" });
}

export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ jobId: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { jobId } = await params;
    const body = await req.json();
    const { action, progress, workerId, runId, error } = body as {
      action: "running" | "progress" | "heartbeat" | "complete" | "fail";
      progress?: Partial<JobProgress>;
      workerId?: string;
      runId?: string;
      error?: string;
    };

    switch (action) {
      case "running":
        markJobRunning(jobId);
        break;
      case "progress":
        if (progress) updateJobProgress(jobId, progress);
        break;
      case "heartbeat":
        if (workerId) workerHeartbeat(workerId);
        break;
      case "complete":
        completeJob(jobId, runId);
        break;
      case "fail":
        failJob(jobId, error ?? "Unknown error");
        break;
      default:
        return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Unknown action" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { kind, projectId, priority, metadata, retryPolicyOverride } = body as {
      kind: JobKind;
      projectId: string;
      priority?: JobPriority;
      metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
      retryPolicyOverride?: Record<string, unknown>;
    };

    if (!kind || !projectId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "kind and projectId required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const job = enqueueJob(kind, projectId, priority ?? "medium", metadata, retryPolicyOverride as never);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: job });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

68 /workspace/app-cl2omik5tds2/src/app/api/agents/sovereign/route.ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  runSovereignLoop,
  makeDeterministicVoice,
  type SovereignInput,
} from "@/lib/agents/sovereign-orchestrator";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

interface SovereignRequest {
  projectId: string;
  topic: string;
  // Optional: caller can pre-seed evidence counts per role for testing.
  evidenceByRole?: Record<string, number>;
}

/**
 * POST /api/agents/sovereign — runs the debate→critique→synthesis→verification
 * loop with deterministic placeholder voices. Real callers wire LLM-backed
 * voices through the SovereignInput contract.
 */
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const body = (await req.json()) as SovereignRequest;
  if (!body?.projectId || !body?.topic) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "projectId + topic required" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const mkEv = (role: string, count: number) =>
    Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({
      kind: "build_verified" as const,
      status: "passed" as const,
      message: `${role} evidence #${i + 1}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    }));

  const ev = body.evidenceByRole ?? { architect: 2, builder: 2, critic: 1 };

  const input: SovereignInput = {
    projectId: body.projectId,
    topic: body.topic,
    voices: {
      architect: makeDeterministicVoice("architect", {
        stance: "support",
        proposal: `Adopt sovereign synthesis for ${body.topic}`,
        reasoning: "Structurally clean and reuses existing kernels.",
        evidence: mkEv("architect", ev.architect ?? 0),
      }),
      builder: makeDeterministicVoice("builder", {
        stance: "amend",
        proposal: `with workflow-engine checkpointing per phase`,
        reasoning: "Concrete implementable shape.",
        evidence: mkEv("builder", ev.builder ?? 0),
      }),
      critic: makeDeterministicVoice("critic", {
        stance: "object",
        proposal: `Holds: needs deeper verification`,
        reasoning: "Risk surface around recovery semantics.",
        evidence: mkEv("critic", ev.critic ?? 0),
      }),
    },
    governor: async () => ({ allowed: true, blockers: [] }),
    evidenceFloor: 2,
    maxRounds: 2,
  };

  const outcome = await runSovereignLoop(input);
  return NextResponse.json(outcome);
}

export interface SovereignInput {
  projectId: string;
  topic: string;
  context?: Record<string, unknown>;

  maxRounds?: number;
  /** Minimum evidence weight before synthesis is accepted. V23 default: 3 (raised from 2). */

}

}

// ── Stage runner ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function runDebateStage(
  input: DebateV2StageInput,
  agents: DebateV2Config["agents"]
): Promise<DebateV2StageResult> {
  const stagePositions: SovereignPosition[] = [];
  const stageEvidence: EvidenceNode[] = [...input.evidence];
  let blockerReason: string | null = null;

  for (const agent of agents) {
    try {
      const position = await agent.voice(input.topic, {
        topic: input.topic,
        priorPositions: input.priorPositions,
        cycle: 0,
        globalContext: {},
      });
      if (position) {
        stagePositions.push(position);
        stageEvidence.push(...position.evidence);
      }
    } catch {
      // Individual voice failures are non-fatal within a stage
    }
  }

  return {
    stage: input.stage,
    positions: stagePositions,
    evidence: stageEvidence,
[The command completed with exit code 0.]
[Current working directory: /workspace/app-cl2omik5tds2]

function synthesise(positions: SovereignPosition[]): {
  synthesis: string;
  unresolvedObjections: string[];
  requiredEvidenceFromObjections: string[];
  winningEvidence: EvidenceNode[];
} {
  if (positions.length === 0) {
    return {
      synthesis: "",
      unresolvedObjections: ["no_positions_offered"],
      requiredEvidenceFromObjections: ["at_least_one_position_required"],
      winningEvidence: [],
    };
  }

    // the previous sort silently made the first/highest-stance position win with
  // zero backing. Now we detect this and return unresolved objections requiring
  // each role to supply evidence before synthesis proceeds.
  const totalWeight = positions.reduce((s, p) => s + p.evidenceWeight, 0);
  if (totalWeight === 0) {
    return {
      synthesis: "Synthesis blocked: no position carries any evidence weight.",
      unresolvedObjections: positions.map((p) => `${p.role}: no evidence supplied`),
      requiredEvidenceFromObjections: positions.map(
        (p) => `evidence_required_from_${p.role}`
      ),
      winningEvidence: [],
    };
  }

  const stanceRank: Record<SovereignPosition["stance"], number> = {
    amend: 3,
    support: 2,
    object: 1,
    defer: 0,
  };
  const sorted = [...positions].sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.evidenceWeight !== a.evidenceWeight) return b.evidenceWeight - a.evidenceWeight;
    return stanceRank[b.stance] - stanceRank[a.stance];
  });
  const winner = sorted[0];
[The command completed with exit code 0.]
[Current working directory: /workspace/app-cl2omik5tds2]
--END AGENT OBSERVATION--
